# Link-time stand-ins for the Android platform libraries the gralloc backend
# (src/util/u_gralloc/u_gralloc_imapper5_api.cpp, through libui's GraphicBufferMapper) links
# against. mkstubs.py turns this list into empty shared libraries with the same names and
# symbols, so the driver links without copies of anyone's device libraries; on the device the
# real ones are loaded.
#
# One line per symbol: <library> <FUNC|OBJECT> <GLOBAL|WEAK> <size> <symbol>. The lists are
# the dynamic symbols these libraries export on an Android 12 system image (all of them: the
# linker resolves symbols before it drops unused code, so a shorter list changes the link).

lib libc++.so
lib libui.so
lib libutils.so
lib libcutils.so
lib liblog.so
lib libnativewindow.so
